- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the game.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 5
        - Boldness: 2
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarmongerHate: 3
        - NeutralBias: 8
        - FriendlyBias: 9
        - GuardedBias: 4
        - AfraidBias: 4
        - DiplomaticBalance: 8
        - Friendliness: 6
        - WorkWithWillingness: 8
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 6
        - Loyalty: 8
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 6
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 7
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 4
        - MinorCivWarBias: 3
        - DenounceWillingness: 3
        - Forgiveness: 6
        - Meanness: 1
        - Neediness: 6
        - Chattiness: 5
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: PEACE SURVIVAL FINAL PHASE: 8 turns to diplo vote. Persona unchanged - maximum peaceful posture. Boldness 2, WarBias 1, Meanness 1 signal no aggressive intent to Rome despite their Tarsus bomber buildup. NeutralBias 8, FriendlyBias 9 ensure diplomatic channel remains open. Guarded 4, Afraid 4 acknowledge reality (25k vs 4k) without capitulation. Portugal at 76% - likely peaceful resolution in 8 turns.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Stealth
        - Rationale: STEALTH MAINTAINED - POST-VOTE DEFENSE PREPARATION: Current research Advanced Ballistics completes soon. Stealth unlocks Stealth Bombers for ultimate defensive capability. Regardless of World Congress outcome, need advanced air units to deter Rome's massive conventional superiority (29:1 force ratio). Stealth Bombers provide standoff strike capability against Roman concentrations.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: People's Army (Policy)
        - Rationale: PEOPLE'S ARMY MAINTAINED - EMERGENCY DEFENSE CONTINGENCY: +100% Military Academy production and 5 free Military Academies remains critical. If Portugal's diplomatic victory succeeds, these academies provide long-term professional military. If vote fails, need immediate combat boost to survive Roman onslaught. No better defensive policy available in Order ideology.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Hanoi
        - Public: 15
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: Hanoi offers 62 Influence and 106 Faith for building Pyramids. Establishing positive relations now positions for alliance if I pursue this wonder. Faith generation would support religion/pantheon benefits. Public friendship helps distinguish from Rome tensions.
        - UpdatedTurn: 54
        
        ### Ragusa
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Ragusa declared war on me (Turn 180) following their conflict with The Ottomans, though peace was subsequently negotiated. They are now at -20 influence and broke my pledge of protection. While currently at peace, they sided with my enemies and must be treated with hostility. -30/-50 reflects this breach of trust and positions me to respond aggressively if they join against me again.
        - UpdatedTurn: 182
        
        ### Greece
        - Public: 5
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: GREECE FURTHER REDUCTION: Greece at 100% war weariness, losing badly to Huns (-72%)/Portugal (-66%)/Rome (-88%). Proposal to repeal Casus Belli helps warmongers. Currently irrelevant - they're collapsing. Minimal positive 5/0 prevents joining Rome/Austria while acknowledging they're effectively neutralized by three-front war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 359
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: PERSIA VASSAL CAPITULATION: Persia has fully capitulated to Rome and is now a Roman vassal. Darius has betrayed our vassal relationship (previously 95/95) and is actively attacking my territory near Susa and Ecbatana with Gatling Guns and Riflemen. This represents a complete reversal - former buffer is now hostile enemy from southeast. Public 0 for potential future reconciliation, Private -30 acknowledges the betrayal and current hostilities. Combined with Rome's domination, I face a three-front war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 443
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: OTTOMANS ALLIANCE MAINTAINED: Strong Order ideology ally providing eastern flank security. Battle-tested at Linz against Austria. Their 6102 military strength and shared ideology ensures World Congress voting alignment. Critical buffer against Austria and potential Roman aggression from east. No change needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 462
        
        ### The Huns
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: HUNS WESTERN BUFFER MAINTAINED: 80/80 relationship continues. Huns provide western pressure on Rome through defensive pact with Portugal. Their 4130 military strength and Order ideology helps contain Rome's 26101 strength. Shared Order ensures World Congress alignment. No change needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 462
        
        ### Malacca
        - Public: -80
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: MALACCA EMERGENCY HOSTILITY: Malacca (Roman allied city-state with 366 influence to Rome) declared war on me Turn 463, reducing my influence from 35 to -60 and breaking my pledge. This represents active Roman aggression through proxy. -80/-80 signals this is unacceptable aggression. However, cannot attack Malacca directly as that would give Rome war justification and tank my diplo standing before World Congress vote. Must endure defensive war, secure Portugal victory, hope war ends with diplomatic resolution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 464
        
        ### Okilis
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: OKILIS RE-ALLIANCE: Okilis fluctuated wildly (717 -> 50 -> 160) but is now my ally again. Increased to 90/85 to solidify this critical eastern buffer. Okilis provides naval support and city-state alliance benefits. Cannot afford to lose ally while Rome attacks from west and Persian vassal attacks from southeast. Stability in Okilis relationship essential.
        - UpdatedTurn: 470
        
        ### Rome
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: ROME ESCALATING AGGRESSION: Roman Special Forces paradropped deep into my territory, pillaging plantations and railroads near Nineveh. They killed my Heavy Bomber despite fighter interception. Rome's 28,575 military vs my 1,528 strength with only 3 turns until World Congress vote. Public 0 keeps peace door technically open (though unlikely), Private -50 reflects reality of overwhelming hostile invasion. They know Portugal victory ends their conquest plans, so they're trying to destroy me before vote.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: PORTUGAL MAXIMUM SUPPORT: 3 turns until World Congress vote. Portugal at 29/43 delegates (67%). My 7 delegates supporting Portugal could provide decisive margin. Rome-Portugal peace deal means Rome is free to focus on me, making Portuguese victory even more critical for my survival. Maximum 100/100 relationship ensures diplomatic alignment and potentially influences delegate distribution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Austria
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: AUSTRIA HOSTILE REDUCTION: Austria continues attacking my eastern flank with Riflemen and Light Tanks, pillaging tiles near Nimrud. They have 16 turns remaining on peace treaty but are violating spirit of peace with aggressive incursions. Reduced to 40/40 from 45/45 to signal disapproval of their aggression while acknowledging formal peace prevents escalation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Yamatai
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: YAMATAI PORTUGUESE ALIGNMENT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active with Portugal at 1351 influence (Ally). Rome-Portugal peace creates uncertainty about Portuguese victory path. Increasing to 95/90 ensures Yamatai remains firmly in Portuguese bloc. Every delegate critical for Portugal reaching 43 needed for World Leader.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Perge
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: PERGE CRITICAL SUPPORT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active, Portugal at 1405 influence (Ally). Perge delegate could be decisive in close vote. 90/85 maintains strong alignment with Portuguese diplomatic victory effort. Cannot afford any city-state flipping to Rome/Austria bloc with only 3 turns remaining.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Heidabir
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: HEIDABIR PORTUGUESE SUPPORT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active, Portugal at 639 influence (Ally). Heidabir at 90/85 to ensure stable delegate for Portugal. With Rome now free to pursue diplomatic victory after peace with Portugal, every allied city-state delegate becomes precious for maintaining Portuguese plurality.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: WORLD CONGRESS VOTE IN PROGRESS - MAINTAIN MAXIMUM DEFENSE: Turn 480 is the voting turn. Portugal at 67% (29/43 delegates) with my 7 delegate support. Crisis-level military situation continues - Rome's 44,672 strength attacking Nineveh (14,262 enemy vs 1,392 friendly in hedgehog defense). Successfully held Nineveh this turn despite massive assaults. Heavy Bomber and Fighter production from Nimrud/Gordium/Ecbatana must continue. Cannot change strategies mid-vote - maintaining UnitedNations grand strategy with Defense 100, CityDefense 100, UseNuke 90, Antiair 100, Air 85. If Portugal wins, war should end with diplomatic resolution. If vote fails, will need immediate pivot to Science/Spaceship with existing nuclear deterrent.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
        
        ### Flavors
        - Offense: 5
        - Defense: 100

          - Sanction: Rome:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Perge:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Greek Sphere of Influence: Harappa:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Hunnic Sphere of Influence: Melbourne:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 469
          - Open Door: Hanoi:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Roman Sphere of Influence: Lutetia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Hunnic Sphere of Influence: Salem:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 480
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 480
          - Sanction: Assyria:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Heidabir:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 469
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Hegemony:
            - Description: Designate a player that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a Civilization receives support from enough Delegates in the Congress, that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory. The number of Delegates needed depends on the number of Civilizations and City-States in the game, and can be seen on the World Congress screen.
              If no Civilization receives enough support to win, the two Civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional Delegates to use in future sessions.
              May not be proposed by a Civilization. Automatically proposed at regular intervals once a World Ideology Resolution is active.
            - Proposer: Unknown

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the game.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 66556
    - reasoningTokens: 7717
    - outputTokens: 383
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Losing Nineveh**
            
            I'm grappling with the recent capture of Nineveh by Rome. This development significantly shifts our strategic position and requires a swift re-evaluation of our ongoing conflict with Augustus Caesar.
            
            **Revising Strategic Assessments**
            
            The recent loss of Nineveh to Rome has necessitated a rapid reassessment of our war with Augustus Caesar, especially considering our current war score and weariness. I'm also reviewing our research path, currently focused on Advanced Ballistics, and upcoming policies like People's Army. It appears the "Sanction: Assyria" resolution is active, which is a key detail in our broader diplomatic landscape.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            My focus has shifted to the immediate diplomatic implications. Portugal's overwhelming lead in delegates and impending Diplomatic Victory, especially with Global Hegemony on the horizon, demands our attention. I'm re-evaluating our options, considering the ongoing "Sanction: Rome" and our negative gold income, to find a way to counter their ascendancy or prevent our own demise.
            
            **Re-evaluating Military Posture**
            
            Rome's overwhelming military presence, bolstered by their capture of Nineveh, demands immediate attention. With key cities like Susa and Gordium under siege, I'm prioritizing defensive measures and reassessing our overall strategic approach, particularly given our depleted military strength.
            
            **Prioritizing Next Research**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research path. While "Computers" offers valuable trade routes and carriers, the immediate threat from Rome and Austria's land forces makes "Stealth" a more compelling choice for its defensive and offensive bomber capabilities, especially considering our current research is "Advanced Ballistics."
            
            **Revising Next Policy**
            
            I'm confirming our next policy should remain "People's Army" given the ongoing war and needs for production and happiness, despite other appealing options like Communism. I've also checked the research path and it aligns with prioritizing defensive capabilities against Rome.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Threats**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic position. Portugal's strong delegate lead is concerning, especially with their victory imminent. However, the immediate existential threat from Rome's overwhelming military, currently besieging our cities, dwarfs this concern. Our Autocratic stance versus their Order, and Austria's Freedom ideology also adds complexity.
            
            **Shifting Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm reconsidering our grand strategy in light of the dire military situation. While United Nations seemed appropriate, the overwhelming Roman presence necessitates a shift. "Conquest" might be the answer to incentivize AI military production, bolstering our own defenses through their actions. "Spaceship" is also an option for long-term survival.
            
            **Analyzing Trade Deficits**
            
            I'm scrutinizing our disastrous gold situation. With only one trade route active due to sanctions and ideologies, and a severe negative income, our financial collapse is imminent. I need to understand why this is happening and find an immediate solution to prevent critical penalties.
            
            **Securing City-State Alliances**
            
            I've identified the root of our trade route crisis: no allied city-states due to sanctions and the "Cold War" resolution. While one trade route persists from a prior alliance, its expiry spells financial doom. I must forge new city-state alliances immediately to survive.
            
            **Securing City-State Allies**
            
            I'm urgently trying to secure a city-state alliance but my gold reserves are insufficient for direct influence. Fortunately, I can leverage our spies. Agent Edimmu recently succeeded in rigging an election, granting us significant influence with Okilis. However, Portugal's immense wealth allows them to maintain dominance, making direct competition difficult.
            
            **Leveraging Existing Friendships**
            
            I'm exploring our current diplomatic standings. While I was initially concerned about Portugal, we have active Declarations of Friendship with them, the Ottomans, and the Huns, all indicating strong positive relationships. This presents an opportunity to perhaps secure much-needed gold or trade deals.
            
            **Negotiating Peace with Rome**
            
            I'm considering offering Rome significant concessions for peace, as their military superiority is overwhelming. The key is whether to cede our occupied cities; it's a desperate gamble to prevent total annihilation. I'm also exploring ways to manipulate our relationship modifier with them to secure a favorable outcome.
            
            **Offering Peace Deals**
            
            I am now focused on securing peace with both Rome and Austria. My current diplomatic approach, characterized by high friendliness and low meanness, should facilitate this. I'm exploring the possibility of increasing our relationship modifiers to trigger AI peace proposals, prioritizing survival over territorial gains from this conflict.
            
            **Pivoting Grand Strategy**
            
            My focus has shifted. Given our strong science and production output, and the improbability of a Diplomatic Victory due to sanctions and Portugal's dominance, I'm now prioritizing a Science Victory. The "Spaceship" grand strategy appears most suitable for achieving this, by focusing on science, food, and production.
            
            **Adjusting Strategy Priorities**
            
            I'm now prioritizing a Science Victory and have updated our Grand Strategy accordingly. This involves maximizing science, food, and production output to build spaceship components, and encouraging settlement. I'm carefully considering the ethical implications of potential weaponization, especially regarding nuclear capabilities.
            
            **Refining AI Directives**
            
            I'm fine-tuning the AI's strategic priorities. Based on ethical considerations and the current military landscape, I'm significantly reducing nuclear weapon development and usage while maximizing defensive capabilities, especially anti-air. Gold generation and science remain paramount for our ongoing pursuit of a Science Victory.
            
            **Setting AI Flavors**
            
            I'm now meticulously adjusting AI directives, solidifying our commitment to a Science Victory via the "Spaceship" grand strategy. Ethical concerns dictate a complete de-prioritization of nuclear capabilities; our focus will be on bolstering defense, anti-air, gold, and science to overcome sanctions and military threats.
            
            **Setting Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm now focused on establishing favorable relationships with Rome and Austria to secure peace. By setting highly positive diplomatic modifiers for both, I aim to encourage our AI to actively seek and accept peace treaties, thereby ending the current conflicts and preserving our remaining territories.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Options**
            
            My current thoughts are on refining our policy choices. "People's Army" offers significant production and happiness benefits, but I'm now considering if "Communism" with its wonder production boost or "Hero of the People" with its generation benefits could be more advantageous, especially given our focus on a Science Victory.
            
            **Selecting Next Policy**
            
            I am confirming that "People's Army" remains our optimal next policy, providing crucial happiness and production to support our ongoing war effort and science victory pursuit. While other policies offer benefits, this selection directly addresses our immediate needs and aligns with our overarching strategy.
            
            **Evaluating Technology Path**
            
            I am reassessing our current technology trajectory. While "Stealth" offers powerful bombers crucial for defense, I'm questioning its immediate utility given our resource constraints and need for survival. "Computers" providing an extra trade route, though currently hampered by sanctions, is also under consideration for its long-term economic implications.
            
            **Locking Technology Choices**
            
            I've confirmed "Stealth" as our next research target. This choice solidifies our late-game tech path, focusing on essential defensive and offensive air capabilities crucial for navigating our current conflicts and pursuing a Science Victory.
